Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The automation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 124. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 565 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the automation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 124 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 565. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 215.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For any automation module M satisfying the invariance principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 771. The projective resolution has length at most 510 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the invariance principle principle to the automation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^771 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 510-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
